([ivedha-tech/platformnex-abilities](https://github.com/ivedha-tech/platformnex-abilities/tree/HEAD/plugins/ccw-workflow) · 52 skills, 74 commands, 24 agents, 1 hook file)
- **ccwalk**: Records spec-driven browser tours of your web app into shareable HTML reports (screenshots, per-group video, non-fatal regression assertions). Claude Code generates and maintains the tour spec. ([source](.))
- **ccwatch**: Fast cost & quota statusline for Claude Code (cached, configurable visibility, context bar). ([terzigolu/ccwatch](https://github.com/terzigolu/ccwatch) · ★3 · 4 commands, 1 hook file)
